return "just now";\n        } else if (diff < 2 * MINUTE_MILLIS) {\n            return "1 min";\n        } else if (diff < 50 * MINUTE_MILLIS) {\n            return diff / MINUTE_MILLIS + " mins";\n        } else if (diff < 120 * MINUTE_MILLIS) {\n            return "1 hr";\n        } else if (diff < 24 * HOUR_MILLIS) {\n            return diff / HOUR_MILLIS + " hrs";\n        } else if (diff < 48 bookmarksString.isEmpty()) {\n            return bookmarks;\n        }\n\n        String[] pairs = bookmarksString.split("-");\n        for (String pair : pairs) {\n            Bookmark b = new getTimeAgo(long time) {\n        if (time < 1000000000000L) {\n            // if timestamp given in seconds, convert to millis\n            time *= 1000;\n        }\n\n        long now = System.currentTimeMillis();\n        if (time > now || time <= 0) {\n            return "?";\n        }\n\n        final long diff = now - time;\n        if (diff < MINUTE_MILLIS) {\n            return "just now";\n        } else if (diff < 2 * MINUTE_MILLIS) {\n            return "1 min";\n        } else if (diff < 50 * MINUTE_MILLIS) {\n            return diff / MINUTE_MILLIS + " mins";\n        } else if (diff < 120 * MINUTE_MILLIS) {\n            return "1 hr";\n        } else if (diff < 24 * HOUR_MILLIS) {\n            return diff / HOUR_MILLIS + " hrs";\n        } else if (diff < 48 * HOUR_MILLIS) {\n            return "1 day";\n        } else if (diff < 365 * DAY_MILLIS) {\n            return diff / DAY_MILLIS + " days";\n        } else if (diff < 2 * YEAR_MILLIS) {\n Bookmark();\n            String[] info = pair.split("q");\n\n            if (info.length == 2) {\n                b.id = Integer.parseInt(info[0]);\n                b.created = Long.parseLong(info[1]);\n                bookmarks.add(b);\n            }\n        }\n\n        if (sorted) {\n            Collections.sort(bookmarks, (b1, b2) -> Long.compare(b2.created, b1.created));\n        }\n\n        return bookmarks;\n    }\n\n    public static boolean isBookmarked(Context ctx, int id) {\n        ArrayList<Bookmark> bookmarks = loadBookmarks(ctx, false);\n getTimeAgo(long time) {\n        if (time < 1000000000000L) {\n            // if timestamp given in seconds, convert to millis\n            time *= 1000;\n        }\n\n        long now = System.currentTimeMillis();\n        if (time > now || time <= 0) {\n            return "?";\n        }\n\n        final long diff = now - time;\n        if (diff < MINUTE_MILLIS) {\n      } else if (diff < 120 * MINUTE_MILLIS) {\n            return "1 hr";\n        } else if (diff < 24 * HOUR_MILLIS) {\n            return diff / HOUR_MILLIS + " hrs";\n        } else if (diff < 48 the href points to the complete url. That\'s fine when reading,\n     * but when selecting text you want the full link. This replaces the shortened link text with\n     * the actual url it points to.\n     */\n    public static String expandTruncatedLinks(String html) {\n        if (TextUtils.isEmpty(html)) {\n            return html;\n        }\n\n        Matcher matcher = A_TAG_PATTERN.matcher(html);\n        StringBuffer result = new StringBuffer();\n\n        while (matcher.find()) {\n            String openingTag = matcher.group(1);\n            String href = matcher.group(2);\n            String linkText = matcher.group(3);\n            String closingTag = matcher.group(4);\n\n            String replacementText = linkText;\n            if (!TextUtils.isEmpty(href) && isTruncatedLinkText(linkText, href)) {\n                replacementText = TextUtils.htmlEncode(href);\n            }\n\n            matcher.appendReplacement(result,\n                    Matcher.quoteReplacement(openingTag + replacementText + closingTag));\n        }\n        matcher.appendTail(result);\n\n        return result.toString();\n    }\n\n    private static boolean isTruncatedLinkText(String linkText, String href) {\n        if (TextUtils.isEmpty(linkText)) {\n            return false;\n        }\n\n        // The link text may contain markup (for instance a <span> around the tld), get rid of it\n        // and turn html entities (such as the escaped slashes HN uses) into plain characters.\n        String plainText = HTML_TAG_PATTERN.matcher(linkText).replaceAll("");\n        plainText = Html.fromHtml(plainText).toString().trim();\n\n        String withoutEllipsis;\n        if (plainText.endsWith("...")) {\n            withoutEllipsis = plainText.substring(0, plainText.length() -
